备注：Detailed experimental-section general procedure: 9 (0.190 mmol, 1.0 equiv), pronucleophile (1.2 equiv), and the row-specific PPh3/Cs2CO3/NaOAc charges; Table 4 uses dry MeCN with 6 h addition and 1-1.5 h post-stir, while Tables 5/6 use dry benzene with 1 h addition and the row-specific post-stir time. Product fractions were isolated by flash chromatography.

备注：Detailed experimental-section general procedure: 9 (0.190 mmol, 1.0 equiv), pronucleophile (1.2 equiv), and the row-specific PPh3/Cs2CO3/NaOAc charges; Table 4 uses dry MeCN with 6 h addition and 1-1.5 h post-stir, while Tables 5/6 use dry benzene with 1 h addition and the row-specific post-stir time. Product fractions were isolated by flash chromatography.

备注：Detailed experimental-section general procedure: 9 (0.190 mmol, 1.0 equiv), pronucleophile (1.2 equiv), and the row-specific PPh3/Cs2CO3/NaOAc charges; Table 4 uses dry MeCN with 6 h addition and 1-1.5 h post-stir, while Tables 5/6 use dry benzene with 1 h addition and the row-specific post-stir time. Product fractions were isolated by flash chromatography.

Product fractions were isolated by flash chromatography.
